### Conflicts settled

- **WREN's goal.** m09 8's original second half ("plays both ends") against m10 27 (one goal). Both now agree on one goal, the key (orchestrator ruling); m09 8 has withdrawn the phrase.
- **What the propped service door is for.** m09 8 says "her own way back to the key"; m10 27 and §8 say "his exit and her own way in". Both are kept: his exit (Scorched Earth uses it) and her way back to the key.
- **The cracker.** m09 10 was revised to m10 2's version (orchestrator: m10's version stands); applied as m10's.
- **The one-voice rule.** FA had one per room; m09 6 makes m09's per scene and leaves per room for m10. Both stand, each for its own mission (§5.3).
- **The speaker button.** FA's "Put me on speaker" against m10 38's "[HaX. You're on speaker.]"; m10's wording, now opened by `same_in_your_place` or `afterwards`.
- **`m09_deck_pulled`'s tier.** Neither list names one. Put in m10's Tier 2 (lines only), because its one m10 use is a record answer. Arc-keeper's call. (Superseded at stage 3: Tier 1, orchestrator ruling, §16.)
- **The pre-breach split's start.** FA had 01:50–02:20; m10 has the kit check from 01:45 "until the split", with WREN copying the badge at the table at 01:58, which can't fall inside a split starting at 01:50. §4.0 now gives no start time (after 01:58, back about 02:20); stage 4 sets it. Arc-keeper's call.
- **Which clue each helper replaces.** FA's "skips one step" against m10 11's named clues; m10's.
- **HaX's unasked line.** FA §5.4 had "I should have told them", §4.1 "I sat on it"; m10 stage 2 uses "I sat on it", now in both places.

- m09 imports: 43 (Tier 1 19, Tier 2 24) plus eight `completed`; unchanged.
- m10 imports: 65 (Tier 1 38, Tier 2 27) plus nine `completed`; was 63 (37 + 26).
- m09 exports for m10: nine (`nightshade_shown_file`, `sleeper_named`, `sleeper_shortlist`, `recruiter_offer`, `deck_pulled`, `albion_notified`, `hax_confession_answer`, `recruiter_secret`, `reading_finished`); was seven.

- Export additions to m01–m08 (§3.3): 38, unchanged.

### Remaining inconsistencies (need edits to the stage 1–2 documents, not made here)

1. **m10's phone scenes have no forced-phone-scene rule.** The orchestrator ruled one rule for both missions, and this document and m10's stage 0 now apply it to Ghost's handset and arrested Victoria's secure line, but neither m10 stage 1 nor stage 2 states defaults, done-flags or re-ring for them (grep finds none). Owner: m10 stage 4–5, or a note in m10 stage 1 §13.
2. **m10 stage 1 §14 change 27** (m10 SA:518) still says the copy-for-the-Initiative line "differs from m09's change 8 second half ('plays both ends')". m09 has withdrawn that phrase (m09 §17 notes the two now agree), so the m10 sentence is stale.
3. **m09 stage 1 still words the two new exports as conditional**: §8's lead-in (SA:353, "plus `sleeper_shortlist` if §14 change 6 is taken"), §9 (SA:395, "if §14 changes 6 and 25 are taken") and change 23 ("Add `m09_deck_pulled` if change 25 is taken"). Both are now taken.
4. **m10 stage 1 §0** (SA:7) says FINALE_ARC wins "until a change in §14 is accepted"; all 43 are now in, so the line is out of date.
5. **The split's start time.** m10 stage 1 §3 and §8 give the kit check "01:45 to the split" and the copy at 01:58, but never say when the split starts; FA no longer gives one either. Stage 4 needs a time after 01:58 that still leaves the pair at the service door by 02:10.
6. **m10 stage 2 §2.2** (SE:118) gives Tesseract "Nobody fixes anything until it hurts everyone at once." It is his philosophy, not the mechanism, but it echoes the retired "to everyone at once" premise; stage 7 should check it can't be read as the 06:00 fallback.
7. **Correction wording.** m09 stage 2 (SE:433) says his model "double-counts"; m10 29 and this document say it "credits the hospitals' recovery twice, so it understates the dead". The meaning is the same; the m09 pointer could carry the fuller wording so the two missions' lines don't drift at stage 7.

## 16. Stage 3 changes folded in

Arc-keeper pass, 2026-10-21, after both missions closed stage 3. Sources: `m09_digital_archaeology/stages/stage_3/moral_choices.md` ("m09 MC") §11 changes 1–10 for FINALE_ARC, read with its outcomes §13–15 and the export end state in §10; `m10_full_disclosure/stages/stage_3/moral_choices.md` ("m10 MC") §14 rows P1–P20, P29–P40 and P48–P54 for FINALE_ARC, read with §15–17. "m09 MC ch. n" means m09 MC §11 change n; "P n" means m10 MC §14 row Pn. ### Conflicts settled

- **The Recruiter on the closing card.** m09 MC §3.2's route table gives m10 card lines ("the Recruiter at large", "named", "About half of them"). m10 MC §6.2 and §10 keep her off the five-line card: her state reaches the close through the cells ledger (`named`) and an "Elsewhere" clause (`double_crossed`). m10-side behaviour, so m10's version (§4.6, §8 r15).
- **HaX's line at the database choice on `albion_notified`.** m09 MC §3.5 "This is the same, bigger." against m10's "You burned the Critical Mass watch for one letter. This is that letter, a thousand times." (P41, which both documents list). m10's, since it is spoken in m10.
- **Ranking the clean record.** m09 MC §9 item 33 ranks it last of four; m10 MC §6.3 gives each record slot its own pool, with the clean record in slot 4 after `m09_albion_notified` and a fallback in the Recruiter beat (P22, which m10 lists for m09). m10's; m09's button wording kept.
- **Nightshade's door line on `nightshade_copy`.** m09 MC §3.1 has "his notes", on the talking branches (triple agent and arrested), not after the shown-file text; m10 MC §3.1 has "his letters", on the arrested branch only, while its §7 table defers to m09 §3.1. The word is m09-side canon (what he cleared is his notes, so it is never confused with the Social Fabric letters, S3-13), so "notes"; the branch scope follows m09 §3.1, which m10 §7 cites. Recorded below as an m10 stage 3 inconsistency.
- **`sleeper_caught` on the double-cross.** m09 MC §9 item 25 leaves it to m10; m10 sets it true when she is taken at the core. m10's.
- **Arakawa's wrong-accusation cost.** This document had "one more guard ahead" (m10 change 23); m10 MC P50 moves it onto the county road. m10's.
- **m10's import total.** m09 MC §10 reaches 67 as "its 66 plus `legacy_files_saved`"; m10 MC gives Tier 1 40, Tier 2 27 after `m09_recruiter_offer` became a presence. Same total; m10's split, script-counted here.
- **`m09_deck_pulled`'s tier.** §15's Tier 2 against both stage 3 documents' Tier 1. Both agree; Tier 1.

### Remaining inconsistencies (in the stage documents, not made here)

1. **m10 MC §3.1** (the arrested branch, L91): "You read what I cleared. Then you know I kept his letters." should read "his notes" (m09 MC §3.1, S3-13), and m10 places the line only on the arrested branch, while m09 MC §3.1 and m10 MC §7's own table put it on every talking branch not already covered by the shown-file text (so also the kept-not-shown branch). Owner: m10 stage 4 or stage 7.
2. **m09 MC §3.5** (L240) still gives HaX's m10 line as "This is the same, bigger."; P41 replaces it. m09 MC §9 item 33 still says "ranked last". Both are superseded by m10 MC; the m09 agent's fold should carry the m10 wording into stage 1–2, and stage 7 should use m10's.
3. **m09 MC §3.2's route table** still says "Closing card: …" for each Recruiter route. m10 MC has no card line for her, and gives only `double_crossed` an "Elsewhere" clause; `named` reaches the close only as the ledger's fallback cell, and `taken` / `refused` ("at large, network in place") have no clause at all. The one rule (§4.6) has no voiced or credited home on three of four values. Suggest an "Elsewhere" clause per value at stage 7 (m10).
4. **Name hazard: "Priya".** m10's gallery guard (Imran's friend) shares a first name with m02's St. Catherine's nurse Priya Raval, and Imran worked in St. Catherine's IT, so players could take them for one person. Stage 7 should rename her or give a surname that rules it out (§5.2).
5. **m09 MC §4.7's hangar line on `double_crossed`** ("Kowalczyk took a phone call when we landed and walked off the apron.") and m10's briefing line ("Kowalczyk walked off the apron in Helena. We know where she has gone.") agree; but m10 MC §3.1's tether cost reads "`m09_sleeper_named`, either form" while §12 sets `sleeper_caught` at load only on `taken`. Consistent, but stage 4 should make sure no line keyed on `m09_sleeper_named` alone plays the "cuffed at the briefing" form on a double-cross (m09 MC R2-02 made the same check for m09's debrief).
6. **Stage 1–2 inconsistencies 5–7 in §15** stay open for stages 4 and 7 (the split's start time; "everyone at once" in SE:118; the correction wording).

### Conflicts settled

1. **The cells line's name order.** §4.6 said "the first in the order above" (bait or trace, secure, surrender, m07); m10 S6 §3.2 computes `close_cell_name` as surrender, secure, console, m07. Stage 6 is the authority for the card's strings and their logic, so §4.6 now gives its order.
2. **"RETAIN" or "retain".** The brief and m10 S6-20 write "Disposition: retain. Operational value."; both missions' printed documents read "Disposition: RETAIN. Operational value." (m09 `albion_folder`; m10 R2-08). This file quotes the printed form.
3. **The card's length.** §4.6 had a four-line card when no human line applied; stage 6's telescope fallback makes it five lines on every ending but Join.
4. **The human line's order.** §13 item 36's "dispatch, Albion, the forty-seven, Elena Torres" stands, with the forty-seven applying only while some were unreached (S6-11, ruled by the orchestrator).

### Conflicts settled (the contract wins)

1. **Netherton naming the player.** m10's 7a contract allowed only HaX and Tesseract to say "Agent" or "0x00"; R1 R-1 ruled that wrong (voice bible; m08). Rule now: SAFETYNET people and Tesseract may use the designation; nobody uses a personal name or a gendered word (§5).
2. **The `named` line at 06:00.** This file had "It's six. The arrests have started."; the ink opens "a few minutes before six" and says "The arrest teams are at the doors. They go on the hour." (§4.6). The aside's line is "The arrests start at six, and no sooner. WREN would hear." (§4.0).
3. **Where `m08_hax_ko` is paid in m09.** This file put it at the debrief; the ink makes it HaX's first briefing line (§5.4).
4. **`m08_nightshade_ko_before_fate` in m09.** This file gave it "Not the bunker. Not yet."; in the ink that line belongs to the no-coordinate branch, and the import chooses HaX's opener (§3.1).
5. **The notice's watch.** This file had the Voltage pair choosing whom the watch waits for in m09; R2 merged the line, and only m10's database list names it (§3.1).
6. **06:00's numbers.** This file listed the partial release among Netherton's lines; R2 A2-01 removed it (with the backdoor and Seattle), and the media story and the $12.8M now sit behind "[And the rest of it?]" (§4.6).
7. **The disclosed epilogue's dark operations.** This file had HaX-style lines on screen ("He's been moved. I can't reach him."); the ink gives Netherton full-form lines in the core's order (§4.6).
8. **The hearing.** One last word became two, by `hax_asked_database`, and her first hearing line is reworded (§4.5).
9. **Farah Qureshi.** "Never met" in this file; the ink stations her on the walkway as `farah_guard` when she stays (§5.2).
10. **Join's Citadel.** It opens on HaX's channel, open on the wall, not her empty desk (R1 A-04; §4.5).
11. **The notice record's answer.** "Nine years late." is cut; his answer is "*pause* Sooner than I managed." (§3.2).
12. **Kowalczyk's taken answer.** "[In Helena. She sold me your name.]" replaces the placing-sheet answer (R3 B3-01; §4.0).
13. **The clean first day** also needs `not m01_player_launched_attack` (§3.6).

### For the orchestrator (not settled here)

1. **`m03_guard_bribed` has no line** in m10 (row 38's Finance lull). Place SE's line as a HaX bark or text at stage 9, or cut the import to Tier 3 (m10 imports 90 → 89).
2. **Two dead m09 imports**, `m04_voltage_escaped` and `m07_team_redirected`. Keep (counts as designed, harmless) or drop at stage 9 (m09 44 → 42).
3. **m10 Join credits**: HaX's SAFETYNET clause is "Handler. Her desk was empty by morning.", while R1 A-04 moved the Citadel scene off her empty desk. They don't contradict (an hour later her channel is still open; by morning her desk is empty), but they should be read together at stage 8.
4. **HaX's messages after `executed` and on Join** are JSON texts with no wording in either contract; stage 9 takes them from the writers' reports or asks for them.

- m09: everything in m09 contract §5.3, including stage 7's `alpha_annotations_read`, plus the read latches `rent_ledger_read`, `refit_ledger_read`, `danny_sheet_read`, `case_file_read`, `legacy_box_read` (§5.5). `hub_quiet`, `said_floor_line`, `confession_heard` and the other file-locals are **not** globals.
- m10: every non-import `VAR` in `m10_carryover_vars.ink` (145: the seven exports, the locals of m10 contract §2.5, and the five `close_*` strings), including stage 7's `guide1..4_offered/_given`, `wren_held`, `kowalczyk_gone_ahead`, `fire_held` and `hax_asked_database`. - `importGlobals`: m09 44 + 8, m10 90 + 9, as §3.1–3.2. `exportGlobals`: m09 11, m10 7. The 58 JSON-only additions to m01–m08 (§3.3) and the two F5 ink changes, then the validator on each source.

### Stage 9 handover

This replaces §21's handover as the list stage 9 starts from. The mission checklists in m09-R §4 and m10-R §10 give the expected result for each check; this list says what to do and in what order.

**Step 1: the asset list, one per mission, before any JSON** (ruling 20, from m09-R M-04 and m10-R §3, §11 item 9). Each entry names its placeholder, so the build never stalls on a missing file and never ships a mismatched placeholder silently. **No image generation until the user approves the scope** (user rule: confirm scope and cost first; one at a time, PixelLab before Gemini). Categories:
- **Characters**: every new speaker's sprite key, walk sprite, talk portrait and visemes. m09 (m09-R M-04): Sorensen (one portrait and voice across her three records), Noor Haddad, Jesse Tran, Cody Old Coyote, Lyle Dillard, the raid team (Kowalczyk, Quintero, Malhotra, Arakawa), and a Tesseract portrait for the clip's person-chat, which m10 needs anyway. m10: Tesseract, Sione Fonoti, Imran Siddiqui, Wendell Briggs, Dr Abena Asante, Pieter Vos, Farah Qureshi (`farah_guard`), and the generic guards and minders (varied). Returning characters keep their sprite keys (§5.1). Existing art at this pass covers HaX, Netherton, Nightshade, Gary, Torres, Vector and Phantom among the finale's cast.
- **Room maps**: m09's ten (m09 RL §1–2); m10's eight, of which the drafts exist at the stated sizes (m10-R §1), with new maps owed for the forward post, the gallery walkway and the core, and the 4×2 server hall (m10 RL §8; m10-R §11 item 9).
- **Backgrounds**: m09's two cutscene backgrounds (Task Force Null's room for the briefing, the Helena hangar for the debrief); m10's closing-scene backgrounds (the forward post at 06:00, the Citadel, the hearing). Person-chat POV backgrounds come later, after dressing and lighting (room-pov-background skill).
- **Object variant art**: m09's two bins on three `cage_bin` values and the trolleys in four states (S4:182, S5:141), and every new object `type` with no sprite under `assets/objects/` (the validator's item-type check); m10's bunker props first (m10 RL §8).
- **Voice-bible entries** for every new speaker before any audio (m09 contract §11 and :620; m10's new cast), and Voltage's display form.
- **The narrator voice block**: a top-level `narrator` entry in both scenarios (m10 has 74 `Narrator:` lines in person-chats; m09's cutscenes and clip have narrator lines too).
